## Surprising Connections (you probably didn't know these)
- `StagePanelInner()` --indirect_call--> `e()`  [INFERRED]
  frontend/src/components/kite/mac/MacStageLayout.tsx → claude-mem/plugin/ui/viewer-bundle.js
- `_format_vulns_summary()` --calls--> `_item()`  [INFERRED]
  code-review/plugins/security-guidance/hooks/llm.py → backend/tests/engines/sterling_kite_engine/test_directional_exec.py
- `_fetcher()` --indirect_call--> `fetch()`  [INFERRED]
  backend/tests/test_edge_signals.py → generate_report.py
- `TradingViewKiteChart()` --indirect_call--> `y()`  [INFERRED]
  frontend/src/components/charts/TradingViewKiteChart.tsx → claude-mem/plugin/sqlite/SessionStore.js
- `OrderWindow()` --indirect_call--> `y()`  [INFERRED]
  frontend/src/components/kite/OrderWindow.tsx → claude-mem/plugin/sqlite/SessionStore.js

## Import Cycles
- 3-file cycle: `claude-mem/src/services/worker-service.ts -> claude-mem/src/services/worker/http/shared.ts -> claude-mem/src/services/worker/events/SessionEventBroadcaster.ts -> claude-mem/src/services/worker-service.ts`
- 3-file cycle: `claude-mem/src/services/worker-service.ts -> claude-mem/src/services/worker/session/SessionCompletionHandler.ts -> claude-mem/src/services/worker/events/SessionEventBroadcaster.ts -> claude-mem/src/services/worker-service.ts`
- 3-file cycle: `backend/app/services/exchanges/kite/__init__.py -> backend/app/services/exchanges/kite/client.py -> backend/app/services/exchanges/kite/session.py -> backend/app/services/exchanges/kite/__init__.py`
